## 5. Emmanuel's preferences and standing decisions

**How to work with him**

- **Food, health and fasting texts follow docs/NUTRITION-POLICY.md** (his nutrition audit, 24 Oct 2026). No cooled-rice
  pass, no small-portion tips for craving food, vinegar only optional, "remission" not "cure".

- **Releases are silent, always (26 Oct 2026).** No update notification of any kind to users: no "New version" / "I've got something
  new for you!" ask, no "Update loaded", no push, no Community post about a release. `loud` in version.json stays `20000101-000000`;
  `scripts/release-check.cjs` fails otherwise. What's New tour steps inside the app stay, each with its speaker.
- **Companions are never "Misty" in general (26 Oct 2026).** Every user names their own companion. Reports, captions and
  copy say "your companion" / "dein Begleiter" / "tu compañero"; "Misty" is only the code default.
- **Updates: a voice note plus a text checklist** with ✅ done, ⏳ in progress, 📝 to do, and the done items stay in
  the list. This is his current instruction (19 Oct) and replaces the older "no voice recordings" line.
- **Post anything that needs him right in the chat** (approvals, sign-ins, GitHub codes, task updates), not only in
  the activity feed.
- **"When I tell you something new, you have to update the GitHub for Maya."** Write each new instruction into this
  file on `main` right away, before building (rule at the top of this file).
- **Human instinct first:** nobody should feel overwhelmed or confused; the app should make sense natively (0.5, item 18).
- Plain, short words. He often speaks by voice and mixes English and German. Name the build, say what changed for
  the user, and give one exact thing to try on his iPhone or iPad.
- He tests on his own iPhone and iPad. Don't ask questions the repo, the site or the data can answer.
- **Tutorial voice: only** `en-US-AvaNeural`, `de-DE-SeraphinaMultilingualNeural`, `es-ES-XimenaNeural`, generated with
  `tools/tutorial-voice/build.py` (edge-tts, rate +0%, pitch +0Hz, volume +0%); never the old TTS (device / Web Speech voice).
  Emmanuel, 24 Oct: "Only utilize that voice from now on going, and make sure it's saved in the files." New or changed tour card or
  What's New text: run `python3 tools/tutorial-voice/build.py` and commit tutorial-voice-data.js + tutorial-voice/
  (release.sh does it for --news). `tests/tutorial-voice-only.test.cjs` guards it. DECISIONS.md 2026-10-24.
- Understand the whole app before changing code. He expects you to figure out how to learn it yourself.
- Small, frequent, visible releases; he wants to see changes live quickly.
- **Ship without asking (21 Oct 2026):** standing approval to release and deploy live autonomously once the tests
  and the release check pass. "I want you to always push, push, push."
- When he says he will test something himself, skip the testing steps for it (he did this for the Settings tutorial).
- He is pausing new ideas while this handoff happens. Critique is not anger: he said his comments are not criticism
  of the work.
- Sign-in should stay simple.

**Design rules and decisions (all still in force)**

- **Back and close live at the bottom center (Emmanuel, 28 Oct 2026, owner request 15):** every back button and every X /
  close button sits at the bottom center of the screen, like a home button, so it can be reached with one thumb. This holds on
  every screen, sheet, panel and overlay (Kochen, Profile, Einkaufen, Essenspause, Scanner, Community sheets, and anything new).
  It sits above the home indicator (`env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`), and it never covers a text field, the chat composer or the
  kitchen bin: when a screen has a bottom input, the button sits just above it. No top-left back arrows and no top-corner X
  buttons. The shared control is `bottom-close.js` / `bottom-close.css` (one round button, 56 px): use it, don't draw your own. Since 29 Oct 2026 it is see-through (Emmanuel: "alle Backbuttons durchsichtig ... die sind so blau", "the X buttons genauso"): no fill, no blue glow, no blur, thin white rim, white icon with a dark halo in both themes; #secX, #whX and the screens' own X buttons follow. Don't give back/X a coloured plate again.
- **Panels that rise over Home must be readable (28 Oct 2026, owner request 15):** a hologram panel (Kochen, Essenspause,
  Profile, Einkaufen) keeps the feeling of rising over Home, but its own backdrop is solid frosted glass (at least 95% opaque)
  and Home behind it is dimmed and blurred, so no Home text or bubble ever shows through. Note body-world-3d.css clears every
  tab background with !important; a panel that is a tab needs a selector with more ids (see section-nav.css, cook-holo).
- It's a game: progress shows as character-style stats (nutrition stats and weight/BMI are being built that way).
- **No smile on Home**: the mouth follows energy (calm short mouth at best). **No glow ring** on Home (the reveal has
  one; Home doesn't draw it).
- **Half-energy rule**: below half energy Misty bounces off worlds; weak Misty can be pulled back in when leaving.
- New accounts start at 100% energy for 12 hours, fading over 12 more; at full power she flies faster, the world is
  faster and brighter, the sparkle trail turns gold. Lower energy feels slower and heavier.
- The companion from the birthday reveal is the one shown everywhere; accounts without one see Misty. Wanted next:
  every Misty account can claim its own companion (birthday, birth time, personality type).
- Home: one page, eight equal bubbles on one circle, no shadows, white joystick icon that vanishes while steering,
  Settings as a glass side panel. Nothing ever covers a ring bubble or the joystick.
- Leaving a world is an Interstellar rocket launch, slow and heavy, then calm, looking back at the world.
- Autopilot rides the strings bubble to bubble with an Ant-Man camera; guides shuffle their lines, never the same
  line twice in a row.
- **Three food photos per person per day**, Snap food and Community together; typing meals is unlimited. Wanted:
  BeReal-style reminders to snap meals (reminders exist under Reminders).
- Community meal posts: the AI analysis (name, ingredients, nutrition, likely blood sugar response, clearly an
  estimate) appears under the photo, and the user sees and can edit it **before** posting.
- Chat room: the **tactical star chart** look, semi-transparent glass, glowing accents, using the existing UI design
  principles.
- Gap shows a dash until a real meal is known. No invented data, no fake activity, honest wording, no health claims.
  One exception: unlabelled simulated users for his test with his three informed beta testers (0.5, item 18). Never
  for unsuspecting public users.
- The tutorial stays current with every release (rule in section 3). The "claim your own companion" step is in
  `tutorial-steps.js` but switched off until that feature exists.
- Any old UI or old design (old logos, old companion art, the potted herb, the old blob with a smile) gets deleted.
- He wanted a What's New note for photo recognition; it shipped with the photo cap.
- **No Food journal (22 Oct).** Emmanuel: "I don't understand the food journal section ... I'd say remove it." Misty's
  sheet has two doors, Forms and Glow. **Saved meals and meal photos live in the Fuel sheet's meal list (today's meals),
  not in a Food journal.** Any task told to show meal thumbnails "in the food journal" puts them in that Fuel meal list
  (task 'Meal log: save photo + estimate, share to Community' already does, via meal-journal.js and sym-home.js). The
  tried-foods tally (herb-quests-core.js journal(), discover(), newFoods()) stays for XP and fair play, with no screen.
  Don't bring a Food journal screen back. Details in DECISIONS.md.

## 7. Do this first (new Maya)

1. Read this file to the end, then `AGENTS.md`, `RELEASE.md`, `TUTORIAL.md` and the last 20 entries of
   `DECISIONS.md`.
2. Get GitHub access with the device code (section 2): post the code and github.com/login/device to Emmanuel in the
   chat, wait, then `gh auth setup-git`.
3. Clone the repo to `/home/sprite/lowispike`, restore the harness (`agent-harness` branch README), install
   Playwright 1.64.1 with WebKit and Chromium, and run `node --test tests/*.test.cjs` once.
4. Check the truth: `curl -s https://therealetm.github.io/lowispike/version.json` (live build),
   `git log --oneline -15 origin/main`, `gh run list -L 10`, `git branch -r | grep wip/`, and the Log below.
5. For each running item in section 6, see whether its release landed; if not, release it in a small task.
6. Sign the cloud browser into Supabase with "Continue with GitHub" when you need the backend; check the edge function
   logs before touching `food-photo`.
7. Recreate the hourly bug hunt schedule if it isn't on your agent. Its memory is `probes/BUGHUNT/BUG-MEMORY.md` and
   `LEDGER.md` on `agent-harness`.
8. Send Emmanuel a short voice note plus checklist: what you found live, what you'll do first.
9. From then on, follow the Mickey 17 workflow (section 0.6): write his new instructions here before building,
   checkpoint often, and hand over to the next Maya the same way.

## 8. Evolution plan (22 Oct 2026): 25 levels, stats, powers, evolving forms

Emmanuel (voice note): "I'm leveling up too fast. We need more levels. After level 10, it should go up to 25." Plus: stats that grow
with level (swim speed, shot strength, world-to-world speed), a magnet power that pulls insulin spikes onto the companion (full at 25,
growing before that), the Home bubble evolves too, and big Digimon/Pokemon-style evolution forms at levels 5, 10, 15, 20 and 25.
One small task per step, in this order:

1. **25 levels, slower XP (DONE, branch evo-levels-20261022).** `world-home-core.js` STAGES is the one table (25 rows, `evo` 1-5 at
   levels 5/10/15/20/25, `EVO_LEVELS`, `evoOf(L)`, `hooks(L)`). herb-config, the care board, levelOf in index.html, Misty's sheet, the
   growth map (shows an "Evolution N" mark), Forms ("next evolution at level 15", "More evolutions at level 15, 20, 25"), the level-up
   card and the profile hub all read it. Fair-play floor: `migrate(xp)` runs once per phone at start (`lowi.lvCurve` = 2); if the old
   curve (`OLD_XP`) gave a higher level, `lowi.lvFloor` keeps it until the new curve passes it (`myFloor`). Only YOUR level reads it:
   `levelOf(xp, true)` / `stage(xp, true)`; members' levels never do. herb-core's highestStage keeps forms as before.
2. **Per-level stats.** Fill `HOOKS.stats = L => ({ swim, shot, travel })` in world-home-core.js; read it in the world swim speed, the
   energy-shot strength and world-to-world travel time. Show them on Misty's sheet as small stat bars. Tests: monotonic, capped.
3. **Powers.** Fill `HOOKS.powers` with `{ id: 'magnet', lv: N, ... }` entries: the spike magnet starts weak (e.g. level 12, small
   radius) and grows to full pull at 25. Build the pull in the cell world (spikes drift onto the companion), with a power row on Misty's sheet.
4. **Home bubble evolves.** Fill `HOOKS.bubble = evo => look` and give the Home bubble a new look per evolution stage (0-5).
5. **Evolution forms at 5, 10, 15, 20, 25.** New, clearly bigger forms in companion-core (today forms follow levels 1-10 one per
   level, and levels 11-25 keep form 10 Majesty). Map form by `evoOf(L)` + level, add an evolution moment for 15/20/25, and extend
   Forms' path past 10. The names already exist: Supernova (15), Galaxy (20), Cosmos (25).

**XP math.** A regular day earns about 248 XP from what award() pays: check-in with a 10-day streak 40, four daily quests 40, three
meals 45, six cups 12, a 13 h overnight gap 36 (2/h + 8 h and 12 h milestones), one 10-minute Move 20, 6,000 steps 30, close the day
15, a full sun 10. A light day (2 meals, 2 quests, 12 h gap) is about 120; a maxed day (all 6 quests + 50 bonus, 8 meals, 3 Moves,
16 h fast with goal, scans, recipes) can pass 600. Levels 1-6 kept their XP; 7-9 take a little longer; 10 is unchanged; each level
from 11 to 25 is 14-25 regular days (tests/evolution-levels.test.cjs pins this). Days = total XP / 248, rounded:

| Level | Name (EN) | DE | ES | Total XP | Step | Days |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Spark | Funke | Chispa | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| 2 | Little cell | Kleine Zelle | Celulita | 100 | 100 | 0 |
| 3 | Glow cell | Leuchtzelle | Célula brillante | 300 | 200 | 1 |
| 4 | Bright cell | Strahlende Zelle | Célula radiante | 700 | 400 | 3 |
| 5 | Star cell (evolution 1) | Sternzelle | Célula estelar | 1500 | 800 | 6 |
| 6 | Legend | Legende | Leyenda | 3000 | 1500 | 12 |
| 7 | Nova | Nova | Nova | 5000 | 2000 | 20 |
| 8 | Titan | Titan | Titán | 7500 | 2500 | 30 |
| 9 | Monarch | Monarch | Monarca | 10500 | 3000 | 42 |
| 10 | Majesty (evolution 2) | Majestät | Majestad | 14000 | 3500 | 56 |
| 11 | Pulsar cell | Pulsarzelle | Célula púlsar | 17500 | 3500 | 71 |
| 12 | Prism cell | Prismazelle | Célula prisma | 21200 | 3700 | 85 |
| 13 | Crystal cell | Kristallzelle | Célula de cristal | 25100 | 3900 | 101 |
| 14 | Thunder cell | Donnerzelle | Célula trueno | 29200 | 4100 | 118 |
| 15 | Supernova (evolution 3) | Supernova | Supernova | 33500 | 4300 | 135 |
| 16 | Orbit cell | Orbitzelle | Célula órbita | 38000 | 4500 | 153 |
| 17 | Quasar cell | Quasarzelle | Célula cuásar | 42700 | 4700 | 172 |
| 18 | Zenith cell | Zenitzelle | Célula cénit | 47600 | 4900 | 192 |
| 19 | Phoenix cell | Phönixzelle | Célula fénix | 52700 | 5100 | 213 |
| 20 | Galaxy (evolution 4) | Galaxie | Galaxia | 58000 | 5300 | 234 |
| 21 | Celestial cell | Himmelszelle | Célula celestial | 63500 | 5500 | 256 |

| 25 | Cosmos (evolution 5) | Kosmos | Cosmos | 87500 | 6300 | 353 |

Floor windows (old -> new): 4,500-4,999 XP keeps 7, 7,000-7,499 keeps 8, 10,000-10,499 keeps 9. Above 14,000 XP people go UP (old cap was 10).
Sim: `node tools/sim/evo-levels.cjs "<device>" <en|de|es> [url]` (floor at 4,600 XP, 20,000 XP = Level 11, 25 map rows, Evolution marks, Forms).

## 9. Food engine benchmark + spec section 14 acceptance (23 Oct 2026, queue step scanfe-i)

Run on main 7b21d55 (live 20261023-051036, every scanfe step through h6 shipped). Engine `food-engine-core f1 2026-10-22`,
policy `lowispike-policy.v1`, benchmark `data/food-bench/benchmark.v1.json` v1 (18 verified label rows: Open Food Facts,
data/shop, Emmanuel's handoff). **The benchmark expectations are NOT expert-reviewed** (expertReviewed false) and are not
measured glucose outcomes: a match means "agrees with spec section 8", nothing more. Nothing was added to the data.

Re-run any time: `node tools/food-bench/metrics.cjs` (text, exit 1 if an acceptance item fails) or `--json`. The suite
holds it at zero in `tests/food-bench-metrics.test.cjs` (5 tests) next to `tests/food-engine-bench.test.cjs`. The script
runs each row through `LowiFoodEngine.scanFood` at the benchmark serving, through the app adapter + Food check
(`LowiSpikeCore.foodVerdict`, what Products / Store picks / Misty use) and through `LowiSpikeCore.rerate` on a stale
stored "ok" dex row.

**Metrics (spec section 13), 18 rows:**
- Policy verdict inside the expected set 18/18, carbohydrate exposure 18/18, confidence 18/18.
- False approvals 0, false rejections 0.
- Nutrition math errors 0 (every nutrient = per100 x g / 100, unknown stays unknown, available carbs = whole label carbs).
- Missed ingredient detections 0 (added sugar, refined starch, coating, sweeteners, liquid, claims, allergens).
- Overconfident claims 0 (no confidence above the expected level, no High without ingredients, no glycaemic load without
  a supplied GI, no "no spike" wording in reasons or alternatives).
- Protein/fat removed from the label changes nothing in 0 rows (they never cancel carbs).
- App path more lenient than the engine: 0. NEIN rows reading ok in the app: 0. Stale stored ok rows of NEIN products
  that stay ok after re-rate: 0.

**Acceptance (spec section 14): all 11 PASS on the benchmark.**
- Variants: every sourced row is pinned to its barcode; Ültje Süß & Salzig has none, so the engine marks
  identity_unverified and gives Low confidence. The live barcode lookup itself is not part of this offline run.
- Serving math, added sugar / refined starch, protein + fat never cancel carbs, glucose vs quality independent (Coca-Cola
  Zero and salami sticks are Lower exposure with mixed / poor quality), exclusions (pork, sweet zero drink, juice,
  sugar-coated nuts all NEIN), uncertainty, 1-3 reasons in EN/DE/ES on every row, an alternative on every SELTEN/NEIN row.
- Ültje Süß & Salzig: engine NEIN (policy.sugar_heavy), Moderate, Low confidence; app verdict spike; a stale stored ok
  row becomes spike.
- Regression: the two benchmark test files run in the suite.

**Per row (engine verdict, decided by, exposure, confidence, app verdict):** Ültje Süß & Salzig NEIN sugar_heavy Moderate
Low spike; Erdnüsse ohne Salz JA nuts_moderate Lower High ok; Pinats Honig NEIN sugar_heavy Moderate Medium spike;
Mandeln geröstet JA nuts_moderate Lower High ok; Feuergebrannte Mandeln NEIN sugar_heavy Higher High spike; Naturjoghurt
JA plain_yoghurt_quark Lower High ok; Bio-Fruchtjoghurt Erdbeere SELTEN incomplete Higher Medium spike; Coca-Cola NEIN
sugar_drink Higher High spike; Coca-Cola Zero NEIN zero_drink Lower High spike; Orangensaft NEIN juice Higher Medium spike;
Basmati NEIN starch_staple Higher High spike; Spaghetti NEIN starch_staple Higher High spike; Vollkorntoast NEIN
starch_staple Moderate High spike; Pudding Vanille NEIN candy_dessert Moderate Medium spike; High-Protein-Pudding NEIN
candy_dessert Moderate High spike; Hähnchenbrust JA poultry Lower High ok; Salami Sticks NEIN pork Lower Medium spike;
Genuss mix NEIN sugar_heavy Moderate Medium spike.

**Notes for the next Maya (no bug, nothing to queue):**
- Bio-Fruchtjoghurt Erdbeere is SELTEN by `policy.incomplete`: its Open Food Facts ingredient list is cut off mid-bracket,
  and the engine rightly sees it as truncated (confidence Medium, reason ingredients_cut_off). Expected SELTEN or NEIN.
- The app adapter judges at the manufacturer serving, else the group portion. A plain-nut product with no printed serving
  and no snack category falls to group `other` (50 g), which is above the policy's 30 g moderate nut portion, so the app
  shows it as "better" (SELTEN), never as ok. With the printed 30 g serving it reads ok, like the engine.
- Gaps still open in the benchmark (from scanfe-a): no whole-fruit label, no plain potatoes, no keto substitute, Desira
  pudding without ingredients, and the Master Knowledge document (scanfe-policy-doc, blocked on Emmanuel).

- 2026-10-22 07:50 Item 35 follow-up (Affiliates scroll glitch, branch aff-voice-20261022): Emmanuel: "Fix the glitch." tools/blueprint/check.cjs timed out on iPhone 13 tapping #toShots on the UGC script ("element is outside of the viewport"). Cause: WebKit on iPhone-sized screens kept an old, shorter scroll height when go() swapped the views while the page was scrolled far down and only then scrolled to the top, so the next screen could not be scrolled to its bottom (e.g. Webdesign recording screen stuck at 0 of 861 px, back on the script at 933 of 1794 px). Reproduced on a bare page with no app code in Playwright WebKit (iPhone 13): swap-then-scroll stuck, scroll-then-swap fine; Chromium and desktop WebKit fine. The Listen card made the scripts long enough to hit it on iPhone 13; the same check also caught it on iPhone SE (LOWiSPIKE) and iPad before the fix. Fix: go() in affiliates/blueprint.js scrolls to the top before show(). New tests/affiliates-scroll.test.cjs; check.cjs now has reach() after every screen change. Before fix: 12 reach failures (SE, 13, iPad). After: check.cjs ALL OK, voice-check passed, full suite green. Not verified on a real iPhone/iPad.

Removed the bundled one-region shop map (data/shop/stores.json, its builder in tools/shop-data/build.py, the town list and the town-name rewrite in store-picks-core.js, the "list covers [town]" text in grocery-hunt.js, the town names in the Shop-it seed sightings in index.html). New in store-picks-core.js: chainOf (OSM brand/name -> rewe/edeka/lidl/aldi/kaufland/netto/penny/famila/salling, Danish Netto by country), parseShops (sorted by distance, point+outline duplicates folded), placeOf/placeFromSearch, lookup(fetch) = Nominatim reverse (address), Nominatim search (typed town/postcode) and nearest supermarkets via Nominatim `q=supermarket` inside a growing viewbox (1.3 -> 45 km), Overpass (3 servers) only as fallback because overpass-api.de took 30+ s today; 1 Nominatim request/s, answers kept in memory per session, coordinates sent rounded (3 decimals for shops, 4 for the address), nothing to our server. merge() builds the general list (REWE + EDEKA) for shops without chain data, with a one-line note. store-picks.js pick view: Share my location -> "Your location" address -> Supermarkets near you (name, brand, address, distance) -> tap -> that chain's list or the general list; denied/unavailable -> town or postcode field (.stp-wq, 16px so iOS does not zoom); "Skip: show the general lists"; chain chips incl. All supermarkets; OSM attribution and privacy line. Saved state holds the chosen shop by name/address only; a shop saved from the old list (id not "osm:") is dropped on load; position forgotten on close. grocery-hunt.js Near me uses the same lookup and has "Find shops in <typed place>". Tests: tests/emergency-shop-location.test.cjs (no fixed town in shop code/data/seed/cache, chain matching, distance sorting, mocked network incl. fallbacks, general list). Browser sim: tools/shop-data/location-sim.cjs (mocked OSM; LIVE_OSM=1 for real answers): iPhone SE (Hamburg, DE, day), iPhone 13 (Munich, EN, night, kiosk -> general list), iPhone 15 (location denied -> postcode 80331, ES, night, old saved shop dropped), Pixel 7 (Munich, EN, day): 0 FAIL, 37 checks; LIVE_OSM on Pixel 7 Munich: real address + 25 shops, 0 FAIL. Not checked on a real iPhone.
- **22 Oct 2026, level-up skin sync + old flowery herb removed (branch levelup-skin-20261022).** Emmanuel: "on a different phone the person leveled up, but the level-up design of their companion is still old ... the old design needs to be nuked". Cause: herb-tamagotchi.js boots and calls refresh(true) before apple-ui.js has loaded cell-world.js, companion.js, spirit.js and world-home-core.js. A level-up found at that moment (XP synced from another phone, a reload) painted the card with the raw base drawing plant() = the old potted herb with stem, leaves and blossoms, plus old stage names. Fix: evolve() waits (250 ms steps, max ~15 s) until skinReady() (plantHTML.cell + LowiWorldHomeCore + LowiSpirit when its tag exists); evoArt() = curArt(m,'hero',p); curArt() never returns the base drawing, only a neutral orb (.lh-orb) if cell-world never loads. Deleted: stem/leaves/blossoms and the leaf gradient in plant(); its old body and pot are now drawn only for wardrobe crops (cell-world still slices the face, accessories, lh-foliage/lh-pot groups from it); the dead lh-guide-herb branch; .lh-leaf/.lh-blossom/lh-sway/lh-wave CSS (herb-tamagotchi.css, beta.css, herb-touch.css); spirit.js falling-leaf effect and .sp-fallen CSS; herb-era copy (leaf dance, flowers, "Hello, new leaves", "Little leaf room"). Migration: readPrefs runs migrateSkin() once (skinV:2): stage numbers as text clamped to 0-9, invalid evoFrom dropped, old skin keys (skin, herbSkin, plantSkin, potStyle, leafStyle, bloomStyle, flowerStyle, design) removed, equipment rebuilt from BASE_EQUIPMENT with only known items. Tests: tests/levelup-skin-sync.test.cjs (fails 4/4 on the old code); updated spirit, bughunt-high-forms BH-012, levelup-style, oct19-old-design-purge-2. Probe: node tools/companion/levelup-skin.cjs (fresh / boot / legacy on iPhone SE, 13, 15, Pixel 7; screenshots in /home/sprite/levelup-shots). Tutorial files not touched (they never drew the old herb). **Released as 20261022-121612** (2137/2137 tests, GitHub check passed, live herb-tamagotchi.js/spirit.js/CSS match the repo byte for byte, What's New EN/DE/ES live). Probe before release: 12/12 PASS (iPhone SE, 13, 15 in WebKit, Pixel 7 in Chromium; fresh, boot, legacy). Not checked on a physical phone.

- 2026-10-22 12:20 Hub B (branch hub-settings-20261022), part B of the split "profile hub" job; parts A (long-press opens the hub) and C (World sound inside Look & sound) run in parallel. Emmanuel: "remove the explore things and have the more important things in there." The profile hub (community-me.js) is now YOU (Profile, Weight & BMI, Weight journey, then a folded "More places" row) + SETTINGS (Day/night switch that flips in place, Look & sound, Language EN/DE/ES buttons, Tutorial, Account) + Delete account in red on its own at the bottom (asks inside the hub, Keep goes back, only the server ok clears the phone: home-settings-sleep-v2.js serverDelete, shared with the old sheet) + the version line. The header gear is gone (it opened Settings, which is now the hub itself). World sound is left out on purpose (SKIP in community-me.js): part C moves it into Look & sound. Explore section removed: Workouts stays reachable by the Home Move bubble; For You, Care board, World map, Gift of the day and Shopping hunt had no visible way in on the iPhone run (Home .wh-zoom and .hs-gift display:none, .nm-pill-fy / .nm-pill-lb hidden, no Finds chip in the scanner), so they sit as small chips under "More places" (EXPLORE_LIGHT / EXPLORE_MOVED in home-settings-sleep-v2.js). Every way to the old Settings sheet goes to the hub: show('page') in home-settings-sleep-v2.js opens LowiCommunityMe first (so the hold on the Community bubble and openSettings() both land in the hub); the old sheet is only the fallback when the hub is not loaded. A hold-opened hub ignores the click that ends the hold (only a press that starts after it opened, or a keyboard click, counts). Footer tip EN/DE/ES now: "hold the Community bubble on Home to open this from anywhere". Part A: show('page') already routes to the hub, so your hold change can just keep calling show('page') or call LowiCommunityMe.open() directly; keep the "armed" guard in community-me.js. Part C: if you remove worldSound from SETTINGS_IDS the hub needs nothing, SKIP just becomes a no-op. Tests: tests/community-hub-settings.test.cjs (+ ui-settings-page updated). Probe: tools/community-settings/hub-b.js (hold 0.7 s and 2.5 s, openSettings, Day/night, DE, More places, Delete ask/Keep, Community picture). The tutorial step that says the hold opens Settings was not touched (voice clips; the hold still opens the settings, now inside the hub).

The companion picture is a plain picture again. Cause of the big black icon: tutorial-voice.css is appended to <head>, tutorial.css is linked later in <body>, so tutorial.css's `.lt-guide svg {56px}` beat `.lt-spk svg` (same specificity); all speaker rules are now scoped to #ltTour and the svg carries width/height 18. Only tutorial-voice.js (speaker markup) and tutorial-voice.css changed. New: tests/tutorial-voice-button.test.cjs (4 tests, all fail on the old code) and tools/tutorial-voice-btn/layout-check.cjs (walks all 70 cards on iPhone SE, 13, 15 WebKit and Pixel 7 Chromium, day and night theme, en/de/es: position, size, no overlap with picture/count/title/text/Skip all, title not cut, on/off states): ALL OK on the code; old code fails it (56 px glyph on the picture). Tests 2133/2133, CI passed, live serves 20261022-121430 and the two changed files match the repo. Not checked on a real iPhone. The cancelled first try (e5bebabf) had left only a clean worktree, reused here.
- 2026-10-22 12:05 Cook > Shuffle > Products: no more spiking products (Emmanuel: "it has even the bad products in there ... should have them in a garbage section, for throw out: 'It will likely spike insulin'"). Leak: fProducts only dropped a product whose STORED verdict was "spike", so caught products with verdict "unknown"/missing (Chips in the old test) or a stale stored "ok" that the current estimator rates spike (Cola) were shuffled. New scan-sort-core.js (window.LowiScanSort): verdictOf(p, LowiDex.rate, LowiDex.rateName) on the scanner's own scale; spike if the stored verdict OR rate() says spike (with no label data the name estimate may mark it spike). Cut-off: only ok "Low-spike" and better "Now and then" feed Shuffle; unknown feeds nothing and is not listed. index.html fSort/fProducts use it (also the enriched PV verdict of a pick), new Throw out section under Products (#ckToss, fTossHTML: name, brand, "your scan", rating pill "Skip it", line "Will likely spike insulin.", x hides it in localStorage lowi.cook.tossHidden, own scans first, 6 shown + Show all), friendly empty state fProductsEmptyHTML (Scan a product / Shuffle the eBook) replaces the old Swaflo fallback, EN/DE/ES ("Aussortieren" / "Para descartar"). Same filter in swaflo-core productPool + shopping (K.lowSpike, swaflo.js products() now carries spikeVerdict), so Buy picks, swap requests and shopping matches for generated recipes skip spikes. Shop it / Cook it / AI cooking / Deep analyze build from recipes or a GPT link, not from scanned products, so no leak there. Cook keeps its dark screen in day mode (data-theme=light leaves #tab-shelf dark), so no day overrides. CSS in beta.css (.ck-toss*). Tests: tests/scan-sort.test.cjs (5 of 8 fail on the old code); mobile-shell Products test now expects ['Skyr'] (Chips unchecked, Cola spike). Probe: node tools/probe-shuffle-throwout.cjs "<device>" [dir|url] [out] [engine].

- 2026-10-22 Profile overview first + one Back (Emmanuel: "one click on account and then you see this overview ... so it's more übersichtlich", screenshot of the round arrow sitting on the Account "Back" pill). Settings > Account, Me and Look & sound now run `profileHome` -> `LowiG2E.overview()` (new: closes any open part, opens Profile) instead of `profileTile('acct'|'look')`, which is gone. The g2e module drops a part left open whenever Profile comes back on screen (`wasShown` guard in pass(); `openPanel` marks Profile shown so a tile tap is not undone), so nothing restores the last part. The round Home arrow (#secX, section-nav) hides inside a part (`html.g2e-on.g2e-open .sec-x`) so the header Back pill (44px, sticky below --sat) is the only back control; on the overview the arrow goes Home and the X closes Profile as before. section-nav innerBack(): system back / edge swipe from a part goes to the overview (LowiG2E.close), not Home. Tests: tests/profile-overview.test.cjs (+ pinned routes in home-settings-sleep-v2.test.cjs). Probe: node tools/probe-profile-overview.cjs "<device>" [dir|url] [out] [engine] (NOTCH=1 pretends a 47px notch): every entry -> overview, every tile -> one Back >= 44px not covered, title not under the top-right buttons, Back -> overview, day mode, DE/ES, Escape, X closes. Tutorial step settings-me left as is (still true; tutorial is being reworked elsewhere).

Item 37b (Emmanuel's iPhone screenshot on 104620: message text showed through behind the top bar and the 'Pinned Message' bar; the tab row stayed dark in Day): the strip behind the top bar and pinned bar is now 93 % opaque in both themes with blur and no fade mask, ends 12 px below the pinned bar, the list starts below it; the item 36 tab row above the room on phones keeps its three rules (rm-bar flex, only .rm-tabs, no glass blur + dark fill) and gets a Day rule (white row, #3a465c text, teal selection, the row's strip takes the room canvas colour); the tab text colour no longer transitions in the room (WebKit left it half-way when the theme flipped with the room open: light text on the light row). Probe now also checks the strip opacity/fade, that the first message starts below the pinned bar, the tab row's Day/Night fill and its text contrast. New tests/community-chat-header.test.cjs. Left for the release step: full test suite, release-check, news.json EN/DE/ES, rebase on main (item 36 release and the tutorial items landed after this branch), release, verify-live --compare. - 2026-10-22 account UI step 1: **Your details**, released 20261022-123232 (your-details.js / .css, loaded by a small inline loader before the recipe-book.js include, stamped with the live build, so the include lines and sw.js stayed untouched; not in sw.js precache yet). Emmanuel: "It's way too difficult to change your personal data ... I'm looking for where to update my weight and I can't find it." One full-screen place (fixed header, one Back), one tap from the profile overview: the first tile "Your details" (g2e GRID), the hero button that replaced "Edit profile", and the hero chips (age / type open it; the weight chip, shown when u.showWeight, opens Weight journey straight on Add weight with today's value filled in: LowiWeightJourney.open({add:true}), prefillVal = newest weigh-in else S.health.weight). Rows: Body (Weight, Height, BMI, Weight journey), About you (Photo, Name, Birthday, Personality type, Gender), Goals, Health (look-out-for, Blood pressure log, Private health details), Account (Email, Password, Community order). Each row opens the editor that already owns the data (Edit profile scrolled to the field, the MBTI picker, Weight & BMI, the g2e panels about / health / acct); a g2e panel opened from here returns here on Back. The grid no longer shows About you, Health, Account tiles (their panels and LowiG2E.open() unchanged). Also fixed: two openers used data-r5="mbti" without data-v, which CLEARS the type (profile todo "Add your personality type" and the r2 mbti row) -> now data-r5="mbti-pick". Tests: tests/your-details.test.cjs (weight in <= 2 taps, every row -> its owner, grid, EN/DE/ES, loader). Full suite 2155/2155, CI green, verify-live --compare OK, live probe on iPhone 13 clean. Probe: node tools/probe-your-details.cjs "<device>" . /tmp/yd webkit [de|es] (SE, 13, 15, Pixel 7, DE on SE, ES on 13: no overflow, no clipped labels, no small targets, every row opens, no errors). Built on main after Profile hub (Hub B, 20261022-123033). The account-overview / back-button task (wt-profile, d3527bfc) had not landed when this went out; its commit cherry-picks cleanly with this one. If it lands after, keep GRID and the hero data-yd buttons. **Suggested tutorial card** (step id `details`, near `settings-me`): title "Your details", text "Tap your weight under your name to add today's weight. - 2026-10-22 14:30 **Released: bug "Your pick: No low-spike products to shuffle yet" (Cook > Products > Shuffle), live 20261022-142600** (main 1c274c0, full suite 2,228/2,228, GitHub check passed, verify-live --compare all same). **Cause:** the Throw out change (3d4b52c, live since 20261022-124009) made Products keep only products whose verdict is ok/better, and the pool was only the person's own + community catches (LowiDiscovery.all, community only loaded once the scanner/dex was opened). With few or unrated catches the pool was empty, and the empty card carried the `in` class, which the phone sheet popper (g2c) treats as a fresh result and pops up as the "Your pick" sheet ~0.65 s after opening Products or tapping Shuffle. There is no "Unlock" button on Cook; that sheet was what he saw. **Fix:** `LowiScanSort.catalog()` turns the built-in shop range (data/shop/<chain>.json, the Emergency Shop chain, else REWE) into Products candidates: real labels that pass the store formula, no pork / sweetener / energy-drink / cooking-basics names, then the scanner's estimator via split() (keep only). index.html `CKCAT` loads it lazily when Products is shown and also asks LowiDex.load() for community catches; own catches weigh as much as the whole range in fPick. The empty card shows only after the range has loaded (or failed) and the pool is still empty, and it no longer has `in`, so it never pops as a pick. Tests: tests/shuffle-catalog.test.cjs (5); sim tests/shuffle-catalog-live.cjs passed on iPhone SE, 13, 15 and Pixel 7 (day/night, EN/DE/ES) locally and on the live site (iPhone 13). Not yet tried on Emmanuel's real iPhone.

## Code error sweep 23 Oct 20:02 Berlin: What's New tours belonged to older releases (live 20261023-201547)
- Bug: scripts/bump-build.cjs kept news.lead and news.steps from the previous release whenever a release's news JSON had none. Since about 14:00, releases without their own tour offered an older change's tour ("Bubbles open in one round circle", then "The Seller Zone now shows real product photos" for account sync and Rooms). That offer marks the news seen, so those releases' own text never appeared.
- Fix: a release without its own lead drops it. A release without steps gets one step with its own news text on its go tab, so tests/news-steps-tour.test.cjs ("Show me" must exist) still holds. Tests: tooling test for a plain bump after a tour bump, and a check that a live lead matches the current news. 2745/2745 passed, CI green.

- Note: app-voice is at about 28.5 of 30 MiB. Prune unused clips again soon, or releases with news will fail tests/app-voice.
- Still open for the user: the youngest age setup should allow (age 1 currently gets through).

## Hotfix 23 Oct ~11:10 PM Berlin: What's New loop (live 20261023-231115)
Cause: three notices per release for the same news. The update bubble showed the news text and marked it seen, but the
Show me offer only checked `toured`, so after the refresh a second "Neu in diesem Update" card came, and Show me opened a
walkthrough with the same header and the same text a third time. With about 40 releases on 23 Oct this chain came back with
every build. Fixed in index.html (guide module: markSeen/markBuild/buildSeen, tick gate, startNews one-step, tour head,
obLater snooze, sign-out KEEP). Tests 2765/2765, CI green, live check de iPhone + iPad (Got it, Show me, 20 s watch, 3 reloads each).
- 2026-10-23 23:02 CEST Simulated user bug hunt (scheduled, odd hour, area **Shuffle / Shop it / Cook it**, worktree wt-hunt-20261023-2302, started on live 20261023-231115). Fixed: swipe back on Shop it (#lsCart, from Cook > Shop it or the Shuffle pick) left Shop it open, closed Cook for Home under it, and the next back left the app. Causes: (1) nothing closed #lsCart / #lcShop on Escape -> new capture handler `cart-escape` in index.html (closes the in-store list first, then the cart, only when it is the top layer by hit test + z-index, stops propagation so the Shuffle pick sheet under Shop it stays for the next back); (2) section-nav.js closed the section when back-swipe.js's Escape (runs first) had already closed the top layer -> back-swipe exposes `LowiBackSwipe.closed(e)` (open-layer count changed, ignoring the nm-f page class any key adds) and section-nav keeps the section on it; openSheet() also counts #lsCart.open/#lcShop.open; (3) the sheetwrap Escape counted the closed #lcShop (aria-modal stays) as a layer above every sheet, so back never closed a sheet over Shop it -> excluded. Test tests/cart-back-1023.test.cjs (fails on old), sim tools/hunt-cart-back-2302.cjs (PASS iPhone 13 en, SE de, Pixel 7 es). Checked with nothing to fix: Cook it hub back, Shuffle reveal, recipe sheet, filters, sub-tabs. Not a bug: one sim run showed Produkte tiles in English for ~1.5 s (frame stall, not reproducible in 3 more runs). Next hunt: Scan + grocery flow.
- 2026-10-24 ~00:30 CEST **Companion one of a kind** (task "LOWiSPIKE: Begleiter wirklich einzigartig + Release", branch companion-unique-20261023, worktree /home/sprite/wt-cunique). Emmanuel: "is it really randomized? Like nobody will ever get the same companion, right?" It was not: the look came from birthday (+ time) only. Now a NEW companion stores `salt` in lowispike.companion.v1 (64 random bits, made once per account in lowispike.companion.salt.v1, synced and wiped on sign-out like every lowispike.* key); seed = FNV("bday|time|salt") -> v2 look: same parts, finer hue (281 steps) + lightness (17), 30 extra names (MORE_NAMES) for v2 only. Sign, element, rhythm, motif, colour family still from the birthday. A record WITHOUT salt = older companion: drawn with the untouched v1 code (sha256 fingerprint of 12,000 v1 companions in tests/companion-unique.test.cjs), re-opening the creator with the same birthday keeps it and never adds a salt. Not a guarantee: no server check for duplicates (would need a Supabase table + Emmanuel's sign-in); simulated: 10,000 people with the SAME birthday -> 0 near-identical, 100,000 -> 5. Names repeat (74 per pool), custom name stays the user's choice. The false comment "two Pisces never share the exact shade" is gone; no "unique" claim in the UI. Browser check: `node tools/check-companion-unique.cjs iphone|ipad [url] [lang]` (mobile Chromium at iPhone/iPad size; this VM has no WebKit libs any more: libicu74/libxml2.so.2 missing).

- 2026-10-24 17:15 CEST Fly-in crash FOUND and fixed (task "find and fix the fly-in crash", released 20261024-174511 quietly, checked on live with the probe: Chromium iPhone 13 + iPad, WebKit iPhone 13 all no reload mid-use, merged on coming back). Emmanuel ~10:40 "I tried to fly into a world and it crashed", didn't know how. **Cause:** the account sync (23 Oct) reloaded the whole page mid-use. With his iPhone and iPad on one account, arriving in a world writes the guide's memory (lowi.guides.v1, a synced key; cell worlds, herbs and the wardrobe call backupNow too), the backup 5 s later (or 1.5 s for backupNow) saw the other device's copy, merged and ran `location.reload()` 60 ms later: the app restarts at the splash right after flying in. Reproduced on live with `tools/fly-in-sync-probe.cjs` (fake cloud plays the iPad): page reloaded 9 s after starting the fly-in. **Fix (index.html sync block):** background syncs ("backup", "now") never reload; when the other device wrote they stop (no push over it, local data kept) and `syncWaiting` holds the merge until the app is hidden (merge + reload out of sight) or comes back to the front (merge + the one reload with "Up to date with your other device", as before). Sign out uses `backupNow(true)` = "final", the old straight sync. Test: tests/fly-in-sync-no-reload.test.cjs runs the real sync block in a vm (fails on old code: 3 of 5). Rule: never call location.reload() from a background path while the user may be mid-flight.
  Also hunted (no crash found, all live, simulated only, VM load 30-60): tap into worlds iPhone 13 Chromium + WebKit (WebKit only the sim's "Failed to start the audio device"), fly-into-world probe every way in/out WebKit iPhone 13 and Chromium iPad (no frame errors, world kept drawing), context loss + restore mid fly-in iPhone SE, old step-aside build 101436 tap run (no crash either). Renderer weight per world: Home ~278k triangles / 31 draw calls, worlds 19k-51k.

- 2026-10-28 food rating fix (Emmanuel's Jeden Tag Speisequark screenshots: scanner said 'Nicht insulinfreundlich', '20 g Zucker in 500 g', 'Höchstens einmal pro Woche' while the LOWiSPIKE chat / Master Knowledge v3 rate plain quark JA): LIVE in quiet release 20261028-152958 (verify-live --compare OK; no What's New, no banner). Two general causes, no quark override: (1) Open Food Facts gives some packs serving_quantity = the whole pack (4306188367345: 500), so the whole tub was rated as one serving; food-engine-core pickServing now ignores a manufacturer serving that equals the pack weight (facts.q, parsed by spike-core packG, within 2 %) and is more than twice the group's usual portion, and uses the usual portion (solids only; drinks keep the label serving; the user's grams still win). (2) Open Food Facts tags plain quark/yoghurt en:desserts / en:dairy-desserts, and the tag alone gave policy.candy_dessert (Skip it, 'Süßes oder Dessert mit Zucker'); food-policy-core applyPolicy drops a tag-only dessert for plain_yoghurt_quark when the ingredient list is complete, has no added sugar and no sweetener and sugar <= plainYoghurtSugarMaxPer100 (milk sugar only). A dessert word in the name or an unknown ingredient list still counts; sweetened quark keeps its warning. Tests: tests/quark-rating.test.cjs (8, 5 fail on the old code).
